> **Currency note (EQO-A remediation, as of 2026-11-16).** This matrix is a point-in-time snapshot (see `AUDIT_BASELINE_SHA` above) and is not regenerated from the checklist. At `258bbf6abb6fdbb92ef0185d058527fbb1e47f04` it differs from `PLATFORM_ENTERPRISE_FREEZE_ACCEPTANCE_CHECKLIST.md` in the status of exactly 21 rows: FRZ-AUD-01..10, FRZ-EVOL-01..10 and FRZ-FRZ-11 read `OPEN` here and a status beginning with `PASS` in the checklist (the checklist carries 109 `OPEN` rows at that SHA). The checklist is the deciding authority; do not use this matrix as current status. The `--sync-matrix` option of `scripts/governance/verify_arch_freeze_plan_traceability.py` rewrites only the REMEDIATION_TASK column and does not refresh the status column.

> **Status reconciliation (T-AF2-FRZ-MATRIX, T-IGX-050-D).** The `CURRENT_STATUS` cell of those 21 rows now reads the checklist status at `origin/development` `2ab57e36d` (108 `PASS`, 8 off-vocabulary `PASS` labels, 109 `OPEN`). No other cell of the table changed. The planning columns are in Appendix A below; this is a planning record and promotes no row.

## Planning record (T-AF2-FRZ-MATRIX, T-IGX-050-D)

> **Status: planning record, not a verdict.** Drafted from `origin/development` `2ab57e36d94eae98c962b3d7b09e5a540d8c70d2` by the implementer of node T-AF2-FRZ-MATRIX (ticket `T-IGX-050-D`, `plan_pending`, no approval line). It promotes nothing, closes nothing and certifies nothing: every `PASS_condition` below states what a certifier needs, and the checklist stays the deciding authority for status. Final row verdicts belong to the W7 independent audit.

**Sources, attributed.** `[WP-4]` = EQO-A phase-2 classification of 209 rows (session artefact, not a repo record). `[RR]` = PT-FRZ-RR independent re-read (session artefact). `[PDC]` = `PDC_TO_ARCH_FREEZE_RESIDUAL_QUALIFICATION.md` (PR #612, a draft on branch `af2/w0-dsexc2`). `[Plan]` = MEP-AF2 v2.1 (sections D, E, G). `DP-nn` / `OD-AF-nn` = Operator decisions, quoted as labelled English renderings. Repo facts (checklist text, statuses, file paths) were checked at the base SHA above.

**Status reconciliation.** The main table above keeps its columns. Its `CURRENT_STATUS` cell is now taken from the checklist for the 21 rows that differed (FRZ-AUD-01..10, FRZ-EVOL-01..10, FRZ-FRZ-11); the other 204 rows already matched. The checklist at the base has 108 `PASS`, 8 off-vocabulary `PASS`-prefixed labels (EVOL-01/06/07/08/09/10, FRZ-11, AUD-10) and 109 `OPEN`. Appendix A column `matrix_status` keeps the value the matrix held before this reconciliation. Per OD-AF-08 the 8 labels become PASS with a linked limitation only after the independent W7 row verdict, and AUD-10 stays non-PASS until PT-AUD-ENFA. `REMEDIATION_TASK` cells were left unchanged: a later `--sync-matrix` run of `verify_arch_freeze_plan_traceability.py` would blank them for rows that are no longer `OPEN`; Appendix A keeps the closing nodes.

**Legend of codes used in Appendix A.**

- `PC4` = the PASS rule of plan section D (checklist L36-43): all four of (1) exact SHA = M3, product tree equal to FCS; (2) code or contract evidence at M3; (3) gate or test evidence executed at M3, with a negative test where the criterion is a prohibition; (4) an independent audit row. `delta` is what is missing on top of that for this row.
- `PP0` = tier+ENT manifest only; production reachability is `TO_BE_MEASURED (T-AF2-CLOSED-WORLD)`. The bracketed word after a path is the matrix `PRODUCTION_REACHABILITY` label.
- `historical_evidence` lists `TAG@sha` pairs parsed from the checklist row (historical and scoped, never PASS evidence), the matrix `ACCEPTED_EVIDENCE_SHA` and the PDC requirement references of `PDC_FRZ_COVERAGE_MATRIX.md`. `freeze_impact` is the WP-4 impact and class, with `[RR: ...]` where PT-FRZ-RR corrected it.
- `EV0` = historical or scoped evidence only; no exact-SHA evidence at M3 exists yet, so none of it is valid evidence for a PASS today.
- `NA0` = no N/A route is defined. `N/A - WITH EVIDENCE` needs an evidence file and an independent row verdict; PARTIAL, SCOPED and COMPENSATING are never final statuses.
- `NEG-P` = a prohibition criterion; a seeded-violation negative test must exist and be executed at M3, named at the owner ticket. `NEG-0` = positive property, none required by the criterion text. `NT-<id>` = proposed red-first negative test of Appendix C.
- `required_certification` lists closing nodes from plan section D, then row-level nodes taken from the WP-12 closing nodes of the related findings.

## Appendix C. Ticket-ready plans for the 35 BLOCKS / CODE_FIX rows

Source list: WP-12 `ARCH_FREEZE_BLOCKERS.md` section H (35 rows rated BLOCKS with track CODE_FIX, previously UNPLANNED behind the PT-WF placeholder). Each plan gives ticket, scope, negative test, regression scope and sensitivity.

- **Red-first** means the negative test is written first, fails on the base that precedes the fix (`AUDIT_BASELINE_2` `7960ae7f6` or the train base), and passes at the RW head G2. The tests below are proposals; none exists yet unless a path is quoted.
- **Ticket** is the proposed RW-train ticket: EQO-A `T-IGX-043-*` where the row is in that track (PT-FC1=A, PT-FC2=B, PT-FC3=C, PT-WA=G, PT-WB=H, PT-WF=J), else `T-IGX-051-*` (A TENANT, B SUPPLY, D RUNTIME, F GATES, G TYP, H DEPS-BATCH). Letters follow the orchestrator dashboard; T-AF2-RW-PLAN may reassign and owns the file-overlap matrix and merge order.
- **Sensitivity** is PRODUCT if any related finding is marked product-path sensitive in the WP-12 master rows (`intergrax/`, `agents/`, `applications/`), NONPRODUCT if all are not, UNVERIFIED otherwise. A PRODUCT ticket falls under the development hold and IIAC activation pin handling of plan section K; nothing here edits a product path.
- Rows whose checklist status is `PASS` (EXE-02, CFG-02, CFG-06, CFG-08, TEN-01, TEN-10) are HIST-REQUAL rows; the plans change no status.

## Appendix E. Limits of this record

- Static drafting only: no gate, replay or pytest was run, and no product path was read beyond the checks listed in the session `FACT_CHECK.md`.
- Cells marked `[WP-4]`, `[RR]` or `[PDC]` quote session artefacts or an unmerged package; they are inputs, not repo facts. WP-4 covers 209 of 225 rows.
- `production_paths` is `TO_BE_MEASURED (T-AF2-CLOSED-WORLD)` for rows where no verified path is known.
- Letters of `T-IGX-043-*` and `T-IGX-051-*` follow the orchestrator dashboard at drafting time; `T-IGX-052-*` letters are not allocated yet.
- Operator-only files (checklist, roadmap) are not edited. Their amendments appear here as the nodes PT-D10 and PT-D09.
